# Parity checklist

**Target:** Prism Terminal v0.55.6, commit `cd26356c6463510677377b2a8a2d36058f19f4c4`. This file is the
definition of done for every Flare milestone. Nothing newer than this release is in scope.

How to read it:

- Every line is a `- [ ]` item. A milestone is done when every line in its section is ticked or has a
  recorded owner decision.
- Source paths are relative to the Prism Terminal v0.55.6 export (`core/`, `src/`, `docs/`,
  `tools/e2e/run.mjs`). `core/` is the shared terminal core, `src/` is the app.
- Flare names replace Prism Terminal names: plugin `flare-status`, signal `OSC 777;flare-agent`, tab id
  `FLARE_TAB_ID`, resume variable `FLARE_RESUME_ID`, profile `%APPDATA%\Flare`, e2e variables `FLARE_E2E_*`.
- `DECISION` marks a place where Prism Terminal and the Flare brief disagree, or where an owner choice is
  open. Open decisions are also gathered in the last section.
- Milestones follow `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-17-flare-roadmap.md`: M1 window and render, M2 pty and
  shells, M3 tabs, M4 themes, M5 input, M6 agent, M7 settings, M8 dictation, M9 ship, M10 sweep.

## M2: Pty, shells, terminal core

### Shells and command lines

- [x] Shells are detected once per run and cached; a new shell is picked up on restart (core/main/shells.ts detectShells). (M2: the_cache_asks_the_probes_once)
- [x] Each probe (where, wsl) has an 8 s timeout, is hidden and async, never blocking the UI (shells.ts run()). (M2: real_probe_times_out_and_kills_the_child, no_unmarked_blocking_calls_in_src)
- [x] PowerShell 7 is the first absolute path from `where pwsh.exe` not in the current directory; none means no pwsh entry (shells.ts pwshPath; shells.test). (M2: pwsh_path_*)
- [x] Shell list order and ids: pwsh "PowerShell 7", powershell "Windows PowerShell" (full System32 path), cmd "Command Prompt", then one `wsl-<name>` "WSL: <name>" per distro with args `-d <name> --cd .` (shells.ts). (M2: lists_pwsh_powershell_cmd_in_that_order, wsl_distros_are_decoded_from_utf16le)
- [x] WSL list: `wsl -l -q` decoded UTF-16LE, NULs stripped, only lines matching `^[\w.-]+$` kept (shells.ts parseWslList; shells.test). (M2: parse_wsl_list_*, wsl_distros_are_decoded_from_utf16le)
- [x] Prism Terminal v0.55.6 does NOT detect Git Bash: shells.ts lists only pwsh, powershell, cmd and WSL distros (no `bash.exe` probe anywhere in core/main). The design spec's "Git Bash, as Prism Terminal detects them" is therefore not parity. DECISION: open decision O21. (M2: probes_exactly_where_pwsh_and_wsl_list_and_never_git_bash (O21))
- [x] Windows tools (where, powershell, cmd, wsl) start by full System32 path, never a bare name (core/main/sysTools.ts SYS; shells.test). (M2: every_windows_tool_lives_under_system32_never_a_bare_name, the_probe_commands_are_full_system32_paths)
- [x] Saved shell id resolves via shellById: saved, else pwsh, else powershell, else first; the UI names a shell only by id (shells.ts shellById; shells.test). (M2: shell_by_id_*)
- [x] PowerShell 7 startup: `-NoLogo -NoExit -Command` with PredictionSource History, `EnableScreenReaderMode:$false` in try/catch, then file style and prompt hook (shells.ts). (M2: bootstrap_args_are_exact)
- [x] Windows PowerShell startup: `-NoLogo -NoExit -Command` with file style and prompt hook only (shells.ts). (M2: bootstrap_args_are_exact)
- [x] cmd has no arguments; its folder report rides the PROMPT environment variable (shells.ts; terminal.ts ptyEnv). (M2: bootstrap_args_are_exact, cmd_prompt_*)
- [x] PS prompt hook wraps the existing prompt once (captured in a global), falls back to `PS> `, prints OSC 9;9 only for the FileSystem provider (core/main/termPrompt.ts; termPrompt.test). (M2: ps_prompt_hook_*, powershell_reports_its_folder_with_osc_9_9)
- [x] cmd PROMPT is `ESC]9;9;$P ESC\` followed by the user's PROMPT or `$P$G` (termPrompt.ts cmdPrompt; termPrompt.test). (M2: cmd_prompt_*, prompt_reaches_cmd_*)
- [x] PS file style (where `$PSStyle` exists): directories bold blue text, no background; executables, symlinks, extension colours, table header, custom header label and FormatAccent cleared (termPrompt.ts; termPrompt.test). (M2: ps_file_style_*)
- [x] parseOsc9 reads only payloads starting `9;`, strips one pair of quotes, accepts only absolute drive or UNC paths; ConEmu progress is not a report (core/shared/termCwd.ts; termCwd.test). (M2: parse_osc9_*)
- [x] decideFollow classifies a cwd against a root as same, inside or outside, case-insensitive, slash-normalised (termCwd.ts; termCwd.test). (M2: decide_follow_*)
- [x] cdCommand: `Set-Location -LiteralPath '<path>'` plus CR for pwsh and powershell; `cd /d "<path>"` plus CR for cmd, refused if the path holds `%` or `"`; nothing for WSL or bash (termCwd.ts; termCwd.test). (M2: cd_command_*)
- [x] PowerShell path quoting doubles the single quote and the curly quotes U+2018, U+2019, U+201A, U+201B (termCwd.ts; termCwd.test #7). (M2: ps_quote_*)
- [x] The cd line is written only at an idle prompt with nothing typed and no agent running (core/renderer/lib/termActivity.ts idleAtPrompt; TerminalPanel OSC 9 handler). DECISION: open decision O1. (M2: cd_writes_the_line_for_powershell_and_nothing_for_wsl, cd_is_not_typing, the_cd_line_moves_a_powershell; the idle-prompt guard is flare-agent (deferred to M6))
- [x] Environment for every pty: `TERM=xterm-256color`, `COLORTERM=truecolor`; NO_COLOR removed; FORCE_COLOR removed only when 0, false or none (core/main/terminal.ts ptyEnv; terminal.test). (M2: answers_for_what_the_panel_can_display, drops_the_variables_that_would_silence_colour, keeps_force_color_that_asks_for_more, the_environment_reaches_the_shell)
- [x] Session identity markers are removed: CLAUDECODE, CLAUDE_CODE_CHILD_SESSION, CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID, CLAUDE_CODE_ENTRYPOINT, CLAUDE_CODE_EXECPATH, CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_SOCKET, CLAUDE_CODE_MESSAGING_TOKEN, CLAUDE_PID, CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA, CLAUDE_EFFORT, CODEX_COMPANION_SESSION_ID, CODEX_COMPANION_TRANSCRIPT_PATH; other CLAUDE_CODE_* config is kept (terminal.ts SESSION_MARKERS; terminal.test). (M2: never_passes_on_another_sessions_identity, keeps_claude_code_configuration)
- [x] Everything else in the inherited environment passes through untouched (terminal.test). (M2: passes_everything_else_through)
- [x] FORCE_HYPERLINK is never set; a user's own value passes through, 0 included (terminal.ts ptyEnv; terminal.test). (M2: never_forces_hyperlinks, keeps_the_users_force_hyperlink, force_hyperlink_has_one_spelling)
- [x] Each shell gets its own random tab token `FLARE_TAB_ID`; an inherited value is never kept (terminal.ts ptyEnv; terminal.test #323). (M2: names_the_tab_and_never_keeps_an_inherited_one, every_shell_gets_its_own_tab_token)
- [x] Spawn cwd is the tab's root; default size 80 x 24; a size asked for before the shell existed is applied at birth (terminal.ts spawnPending, desiredSize). (M2: a_size_asked_for_before_the_shell_existed_is_applied_at_birth, resize_before_the_shell_exists_is_applied_at_birth, a_missing_folder_is_an_error_not_home)
- [x] A spawn for an id that is live or pending is refused, the first wins (terminal.ts spawnTerm; terminal.sessions.test). (M2: a_spawn_for_a_live_id_is_refused, a_second_spawn_for_a_pending_id_is_refused)
- [x] A tab closed while its shell starts: no shell starts, or the started one is killed and nothing registered (terminal.ts killedWhilePending; terminal.sessions.test). (M2: a_tab_closed_while_its_shell_starts_starts_no_shell)
- [x] Warm shells: at most two, one per tab root (case-insensitive), started ahead for the active tab; adopted only when shell kind and plugin answer match; banner replay capped at 65536 chars; never adopted for a resume; removed by identity, every eviction kills (terminal.ts prewarmShell, spawnPending; terminal.sessions.test). (M2: closing_an_adopted_tab_keeps_the_next_warm_shell, a_warm_shell_with_the_other_plugin_answer_makes_way, at_most_two_warm_shells_one_per_root, kill_warm_kills_every_spare, a_warm_shell_is_adopted_and_the_banner_is_there)
- [x] Shell output is coalesced into batches every 8 ms per tab; a batch is flushed before the exit message (terminal.ts OutputBatcher; terminal.test). (M2: coalesces_chunks_and_flushes_once_per_window, output_is_batched_and_flushed_before_the_exit_message)
- [x] Exit: session removed, exit crumb logged, the renderer told (terminal.ts; TerminalPanel SessionRoute). (M2: an_exit_without_end_of_file_still_reports, the_exit_comes_after_the_last_output, a_shell_that_exits_by_itself_is_reported_and_removed (the crumb is a log line; the diag file is deferred to M9))
- [x] A failed spawn writes red "Could not start the shell." into the tab and stops any resume skeleton (TerminalPanel.tsx createSession). (M2: a_failed_spawn_writes_the_red_line (the resume skeleton is deferred to M6))
- [x] After a spawn resolves the real cols and rows are sent once more (TerminalPanel createSession). (M2: start_sends_the_real_size_once_more)
- [x] Resume launch passes the id in the environment, never the command line: PowerShell appends `& { $r = $env:FLARE_RESUME_ID; Remove-Item Env:FLARE_RESUME_ID; claude --resume $r }`; cmd runs `/K set FLARE_RESUME_ID=& claude --resume %FLARE_RESUME_ID%`; codex runs `codex resume --last`; WSL and other shells are left alone (terminal.ts withResume; terminal.test). (M2: two_resumes_share_one_command_line, powershell_resume_clears_the_variable, cmd_resume_has_no_quote, codex_resume_has_no_id, other_shells_are_left_alone)
- [x] Every shell of a kind starts with the same command line (regression-rules same-command-line, #180). (M2: two_resumes_share_one_command_line)
- [x] Pty options: xterm-256color, ConPTY on, bundled `conpty.dll` and `OpenConsole.exe` loaded first; a startup check fails loudly if missing (terminal.ts PTY_OPTS; regression-rules bundled-conpty). (M2: verify_loads_the_bundled_dll_by_absolute_path, the_conpty_dll_in_the_process_is_the_bundled_one, the_open_console_serving_a_pty_is_the_bundled_exe, a_missing_bundle_fails_loudly_and_exits_three)

### Pty, resize, lifetime

- [x] ConPTY sends nothing on a resize, so the cursor's logical line is carried across it: only on the normal screen and only with no printed text below the cursor line; the line is rewritten at the new width and the cursor put back on the same character by cell count (TerminalPanel.tsx fitKeepingCursorLine; termCells.ts cellFrom; e2e promptLayout). DECISION: open decision O3, confirm still needed with alacritty_terminal reflow. (M2: RESOLVED (O3): o3_* and the resize.rs unit tests; a vertical re-anchor, not a carry)
- [x] Wrapped lines reflow the way ConPTY does (windowsPty conpty build 22621 in xterm); the prompt line returns without holes (TerminalPanel createSession). (M2: o3_cmd_typed_text_survives_narrow_and_wide, o3_powershell_typed_text_survives, o3_pwsh_typed_text_survives)
- [x] A refit skips hidden or zero-size panels; while the room animates the terminal keeps its columns, is clipped, and fits once at the end (TerminalPanel refit; core/renderer/lib/fitHold.ts; fitHold.test). (M2: zero_size_renders_none (M1); fitHold is deferred to M3)
- [x] A refit suppresses output scoring for 800 ms (termActivity.ts suppressActivity). (M2: deferred to M6)
- [x] pty resize rejects cols under 2, rows under 1, non-integers; a refused resize retries up to five times at 300 ms while still wanted (terminal.ts resizeTerm). (M2: resize_rejects_bad_sizes, resize_retries_five_times_while_still_wanted)
- [x] A tab shown again gets one more fit and a full row repaint a frame later (TerminalPanel refit; e2e hiddenResize). (M2: deferred to M3)
- [x] Scrollback is 10000 lines per tab (TerminalPanel new Terminal options). (M2: scrollback_is_ten_thousand_lines)
- [x] A tab switch keeps each terminal alive (scrollback, selection, alternate screen); detached, never disposed; the shell runs on unseen and output keeps being parsed (TerminalPanel detach comment). (M2: output_keeps_being_parsed_while_nothing_looks)
- [x] Kill order: remove the session, dispose subscriptions, then kill the pty (terminal.ts killTerm). (M2: kill_is_remembered_until_the_exit_lands, killing_one_tab_leaves_the_others)
- [x] A kill is remembered until native exit lands: exit code polled every 20 ms for 5 s then once a second; a stuck kill is logged once; a late exit is still heard (terminal.ts killPty, pollExitCode; terminal.test). (M2: kill_is_remembered_until_the_exit_lands, polls_fast_then_slow_says_stuck_once_and_hears_a_late_exit)
- [x] Quit waits for every killed shell to be gone with a 3 s cap, spares included (terminal.ts shellsGone, killAll; terminal.sessions.test; regression-rules quit-waits-for-shells). (M2: shells_gone_*, ten_real_shells_killed_none_remain, kill_while_output_flows_50_times, a_warm_spare_is_killed_at_quit)
- [x] Closing a tab kills only that tab's shell; late output for a closed tab leaves nothing behind (termActivity.ts forgetSession; termActivity.test). (M2: killing_one_tab_leaves_the_others, late_output_for_a_closed_tab_goes_nowhere)
- [x] Throughput: a runaway `cat` of 100 MB keeps the UI responsive and Ctrl+C reaches the shell (roadmap Review Focus). (M2: a_100_mb_flood_keeps_snapshots_fast_and_arrives_complete, ctrl_c_reaches_a_flooding_program)
- [x] No blocking call on a live path: probes, stats and spawns run on workers; blocking calls are marked `sync-ok` and checked by a test (docs/build-and-conventions.md; CLAUDE.md "Main never waits on the disk"). (M2: no_unmarked_blocking_calls_in_src, every_sync_ok_has_a_reason)

### Cells and the grid model

- [x] A row's text is built cell by cell: empty cells are spaces, wide-char spacers are nothing, an index maps cell to text position (core/renderer/lib/termCells.ts cellText; termCells.test). (M2: cell_text_*)
- [x] textEnd is the cell just past the last printed cell (termCells.ts). (M2: cell_text_*)
- [x] An emoji is two UTF-16 units in one cell pair and a link after it keeps its edges (termCells.test). (M2: cell_text_emoji_keeps_link_edges)
- [x] cellFrom walks back over wrapped rows, a cursor past the text moves right with wrapping, never above row 0 (termCells.test). (M2: cell_from_*)
- [x] logicalLine joins a wrapped line from any of its rows; charAtCell clamps to the line (termCells.test). (M2: logical_line_joins_from_any_row, char_at_cell_clamps)
- [x] Reading a row into cells reuses one scratch cell and copies each value out (termCells.test). (M2: buffer_row_cells_reuses_one_scratch)

### Activity, bell, bus

- [x] Typing is heard from keys, never from the terminal's own replies; plain-text payloads (no leading ESC) such as an IME commit count as typing (TerminalPanel onKey/onData; termActivity.ts looksTyped; regression-rules typing-on-onkey). (M2: typing_marks_last_typed_and_replies_do_not, send_text (activity scoring is deferred to M6))
- [x] Input echo: output within 350 ms of a keystroke is echo, not agent work; typing keeps renewing; a session never typed into has no window (termActivity.ts inputEcho; termActivity.test). (M2: deferred to M6 (flare-agent))
- [x] Suppression windows expire on schedule, default 800 ms (termActivity.test). (M2: deferred to M6)
- [x] Typing ends an agent's birth (termActivity.ts markTouched; termActivity.born.test). (M2: deferred to M6)
- [x] An agent's startup paint ends at the first silence over 1500 ms (QUIET_GAP); late detection costs nothing; a session with no agent is never suppressed (termActivity.born.test). (M2: deferred to M6)
- [x] idleAtPrompt is true only when a prompt report arrived after the last keystroke (termActivity.test). (M2: deferred to M6)
- [x] forgetSession clears touched, last input, suppression, resume, prompt, birth and output clocks (termActivity.test). (M2: deferred to M6)
- [x] Bell: at most one per 1000 ms per tab reaches the host; never a sound; the taskbar flashes only while unfocused (core/renderer/lib/termReplies.ts BELL_GAP_MS, bellGate; src/main/bellFlash.test; e2e bell). (M2: bell_gate_*, bells_are_gated_to_one_a_second; the taskbar flash: M10, `flare-win/src/taskbar_flash.rs` and `a_bell_asks_the_taskbar_to_flash_and_a_burst_asks_once`)
- [x] The bus publishes only: keys pressed (for the question indicator, nothing kept), the shell's folder, its title, the agent's hook signal, which tabs are resuming, and the visible bottom text rows (core/renderer/lib/termBus.ts; regression-rules replies-only-when-asked). Flare uses typed channels. (M2: typed TermEvent: drain_events carries only the listed kinds)
- [x] The app answers a program only what it asked: OSC 52 reads are never answered; colour and version replies only on request (core/renderer/components/termReplyHandlers.ts; termReplies.ts; regression-rules replies-only-when-asked). (M2: osc52_*, osc_colour_*, colour_query_*, dsr_theme_reply_values, decrqm_reply_values)
- [x] XTVERSION: CSI > q answers the terminal's name, version and renderer, and the colours, only to the asker (PRIVACY.md; e2e XTVERSION; core/shared/termVersion.ts; termVersion.test). DECISION: confirm the Flare name string and format. (M2: xtversion_*; the Flare name string is still an owner question)
- [x] Program-selection copy notice: a drag that changes the clipboard shows "Copied" once, a late copy is heard, a plain click or unchanged clipboard is silent, a double click counts, a mouse-owning terminal is not watched (core/renderer/lib/appCopyNotice.ts; appCopyNotice.test). (M2: the 9 app_copy tests (app_copy.rs, Task 12))
- [x] Diagnostics never hold typed text, clipboard or audio (design spec Constraints). (M2: deferred to M9 (flare-diag))

## M3: Tabs, restore, single instance, window state

### Tab model and strip

- [x] A tab is one shell plus its folder; two tabs may share a folder; a folder may be opened again as a new tab (src/renderer/src/lib/tabs.ts addTab; tabs.test). (M3: allows_two_tabs_in_the_same_folder, adds_at_the_end_and_activates)
- [x] A new tab is appended and becomes active (tabs.ts addTab). (M3: adds_at_the_end_and_activates)
- [x] Closing the active tab focuses the right neighbour, else the left, else nothing; closing a background tab keeps the active one (tabs.ts closeTab; tabs.test). (M3: closing_the_active_tab_hands_over_right_then_left, closing_a_background_tab_keeps_the_active_one, closing_the_last_tab_leaves_nothing_active)
- [x] Ctrl+Tab / Ctrl+Shift+Tab step next and previous with wrap (tabs.ts stepTab; tabs.test; App.tsx 'next-tab'). (M3: steps_with_wrap, ctrl_tab_in_order_wraps)
- [x] Ctrl+1 to Ctrl+9 select tab 1 to 9; a number past the last does nothing and the key is not taken (keymap.ts 'jump-tab'). (M3: jump_tab_takes_1_to_9_only_without_shift, jump_takes_one_to_nine_and_a_number_past_the_last_is_not_taken)
- [x] Reorder by dragging the tab with the pointer, not HTML5 drag; a press must travel 4 px (DRAG_SLOP) before it is a drag, below that it is a click (src/renderer/src/lib/chromeLayout.ts; components/useTabReorder.ts). (M3: the_slop_is_four_pixels_exactly, dragging_the_first_tab_moves_it_and_not_the_window)
- [x] The carried tab is clamped to its row or column; neighbours slide 170 ms (CARRY_SLIDE); box geometry is frozen when the drag starts (useTabReorder.ts). (M3: the_carried_tab_is_clamped_to_its_row, neighbours_slide_to_open_the_gap, a_carried_tab_follows_the_pointer_and_neighbours_slide)
- [x] The drop slot follows the carried tab's leading edge so first place is reachable even when the carried tab is wider (lib/tabDrop.ts dropSlot; tabDrop.test #125). (M3: reaches_first_place_when_the_carried_tab_is_wider, the_drop_slot_is_the_leading_edge)
- [x] reorderTabs corrects the index for the removed slot on a move right; a drop past the end appends (tabs.ts reorderTabs; tabs.test). (M3: reorders_correcting_for_the_removed_slot)
- [x] Keyboard focus held before a drag is given back on the next frame after it ends (useTabReorder.ts endDrag). (M3: the drag state is tested; the hand-back of keyboard focus after a drag has no test by name yet) (M10: keyboard_focus_returns_to_the_terminal_on_the_frame_after_a_drag, crates/flare/src/appctl.rs)
- [x] Middle-click closes a tab via auxclick button 1, not mousedown (components/tabList.ts auxClose). (M3: middle_click_closes_and_right_click_opens_the_tab_menu, middle_click_closes_right_click_menus)
- [x] Right-click on a tab: "Close tab" (hint = close chord) and, for a folder tab only, "Copy folder path" (components/tabMenus.tsx useTabMenus). (M3: the_tab_menu_offers_close_and_copy_path_for_a_folder_tab_only)
- [x] A tab's tooltip is the whole folder path; while Failed applies it adds "Failed: <kind>" (tabList.ts tabTitle). (M3: path tooltip tested by centres_the_tip_under_the_item and a_tip_waits_a_second_and_is_centred_under_the_tab; deferred to M6: the "Failed:" line) (M10: a_failed_row_carries_the_kind_in_its_tip, crates/flare/src/appctl.rs; a_failed_tab_tooltip_carries_the_kind, crates/flare/src/tabs_ui.rs; centres_the_tip_under_the_item, crates/flare-tabs/src/tip_place.rs)
- [x] Tab label is the last path segment; two different folders sharing a name both get " - <parent>"; two tabs in the same folder keep the plain name; comparison ignores case, slash direction, trailing separators; a drive root is labelled by its letter (tabs.ts tabLabels, folderKey; tabs.test). (M3: base_and_parent_names, same_names_are_told_apart_by_the_parent, a_drive_root_is_labelled_by_its_letter, one_folder_spelled_two_ways_is_not_a_clash)

### Tab restore

- [x] tabs.json (profile) holds `{tabs:[{cwd, agent?}], active}`, written 400 ms after a tab-list report, atomically (temp then rename) (src/main/tabsStore.ts createTabsStore; atomicWrite). (M3: store_writes_the_last_save_after_the_delay, store_flush_writes_at_once, the_file_shape_round_trips, write_atomic_replaces_and_leaves_no_temp)
- [x] Every close, quit, session-end and window close flushes the pending save synchronously (tabsStore.ts flush; tabsStore.test). (M3: end_of_life_flushes_both_files_then_kills_and_waits, the_close_flushes_tabs_json_before_the_shells_are_killed, store_flush_writes_at_once)
- [x] A tabs.json or settings.json that exists but cannot be read is never replaced by an empty file: it is moved to `.bad` before the first write, or, when that fails too, the store writes nothing for the session; only a missing file reads as empty. A flush is numbered under the same lock as the worker's save, so an older debounced save never lands over it; a failed temp write removes its `.tmp`; the restored front tab is the one at `active` even when an earlier placeholder was closed; an import past its ceiling stops copying and stale `flare-import-*` folders are swept (audit 2026-10-20 M3, L17-L20, PT would overwrite; Flare deliberately keeps the file). (M3: an_unreadable_tabs_file_is_set_aside_and_a_locked_one_is_never_overwritten, an_unreadable_file_is_kept_as_bad_before_the_first_write, a_file_that_cannot_be_read_or_moved_is_never_overwritten, a_flush_is_never_overwritten_by_a_save_the_worker_already_took, a_failed_temp_write_leaves_no_temp_file, the_front_tab_is_the_one_at_active_even_when_an_earlier_placeholder_was_closed, a_cancelled_read_copies_nothing_and_leaves_no_folder, a_read_past_the_ceiling_is_told_to_stop_copying)

- [x] One close rule, not a setting: a plain shell closes unasked; a tab hosting an agent asks, working or idle (core/renderer/lib/agentClose.ts asksBeforeClosingTab; agentClose.test). (M3: a_plain_shell_closes_unasked_an_agent_asks)

- [x] Bodies: idle "<Agent> is running in <tab>. Closing the tab kills the shell, and the session with it."; working "<Agent> has been working for <duration> in <tab>" plus ", and N other tab(s) is/are working too" and ". Closing kills the shell, and the answer with it."; install "Installing restarts the app, which kills the shell, and the answer with it." (App.tsx Dialog body). (M3: bodies_name_the_work, agent_names)
- [x] Agent names Claude, Codex, "The agent"; working time is the longest-running agent's (agentClose.ts AGENT_NAMES; agentClose.test). (M3: agent_names, the_window_ask_names_the_longest_and_counts_the_others)
- [x] Buttons: "Cancel" and a primary "Close tab", "Close window" or "Install and restart" (App.tsx choices). (M3: primary_labels, the_primary_and_cancel_buttons_report)
- [x] Window close with an agent working: veto, raise, ask; go-ahead closes; Cancel leaves open (index.ts; App.tsx onCloseRequest). (M3: nobody_working_means_no_window_ask, a_tab_with_an_agent_asks_and_cancel_keeps_it)
- [x] A quit interrupted by the veto is finished by the go-ahead; after Cancel a later close is ordinary (closeFlow.test). (M3: an_interrupted_quit_is_finished_by_the_go_ahead)
- [x] An install answered in advance goes straight through once (closeFlow.test). (M3: an_install_answered_in_advance_goes_through_once)
- [x] One question at a time: while a question or update window is up, tab chords swallow the key except close-tab (keymap.ts underModal; regression-rules ONE QUESTION AT A TIME). (M3: under_a_question_chords_are_swallowed_except_close_tab, ctrl_t_and_ctrl_tab_do_nothing_under_the_question)
- [x] Closing a tab kills its shell, disposes the session, forgets its state; Settings has no shell (App.tsx closeNow). (M3: set_agent_none_forgets_and_closing_forgets, kill_removes_the_handle_and_kills_the_shell)
- [x] On close every shell is killed and the app waits for all to be gone, 3 s cap, on the child's exit code, not the exit event; ten live shells quit three times with code 0 (index.ts will-quit, shellsGone; e2e quitManyShells). (M3: shutdown_kills_every_shell_and_waits, shutdown_gives_up_after_three_seconds_and_says_so, ten_live_tabs_quit_by_themselves_with_code_zero_three_times)
- [x] DIFF-24 (audit 2026-10-20, H1 and L1): closing a tab ends only the shell's console children and spares GUI-subsystem apps it started (Notepad, Excel, Chrome) with everything under them; a process created before its listed parent (a recycled pid) is spared, and so is a console process attached to another console host than the shell's (`start cmd /k npm run dev`, a window of its own; general review 2026-10-23, term-2, `a_console_window_of_its_own_is_spared`). Prism Terminal's node-pty ends only the console's own process list, so it spares those too. The kill-on-close job has SILENT_BREAKAWAY_OK, so only the shells are members and a GUI app also outlives a quit or crash. The flag cannot tell a GUI app from any other child, so after a CRASH a child with no console (CREATE_NO_WINDOW, DETACHED_PROCESS, FreeConsole daemon) outlives it too, where the job once killed it; a console child still dies (the crash test now asserts it), and a tab close or quit ends them all but GUI apps through kill_descendants. Prism Terminal ended every descendant. (closing_a_tab_ends_its_console_children_and_spares_a_gui_app, a_gui_app_outlives_a_crash_of_the_terminal_that_started_it, a_gui_child_and_everything_under_it_is_spared, a_process_older_than_its_parent_is_a_recycled_pid_and_is_spared, crates/flare-term/src/pty.rs)
- [x] Windows shutdown or log-off flushes tabs.json and the diag log synchronously (index.ts session-end). (M3: a_real_end_session_message_runs_the_callback; audit 2026-10-20 H2: the watcher window was message-only and Windows never sent it the end-session messages, now a hidden top-level window, the_window_is_top_level_and_never_shown; end_of_life_flushes_both_files_then_kills_and_waits)
- [x] Ctrl+W closes a tab (match also takes Ctrl+Shift+W). DECISION: PT's label says Ctrl+Shift+W; Flare label should read Ctrl+W, matching the stated rule (keymap.ts 'close-tab'). (M3: ctrl_w_and_ctrl_shift_w_both_close_ctrl_t_and_ctrl_shift_t_both_open, exit_ends_its_tab_and_ctrl_w_closes_the_front, every_label_is_the_key_that_chord_answers_to)

## M6: Agent indicator and hooks

### Indicator model

- [x] One state per tab: working, question, failed, done or none; priority working > question > failed > done (src/renderer/src/components/tabLooks.ts). (M6: working_outranks_question_outranks_failed_outranks_done)
- [x] Working shows on every tab, front included; question on any tab even the front, only when not working; failed only with its switch on and not working or question; done only with its switch on and none higher (tabLooks.ts; useAgentIndicator.ts). (M6: each_attention_mark_is_behind_its_own_switch, finished_is_not_raised_on_the_tab_in_front_but_a_question_is)
- [x] A tab is "present" (agent in it) when the poll reports one, a Claude/Codex title claims one, or a hook arrives (useAgentIndicator.ts). (M6: a_claude_title_is_the_agent_present_ahead_of_the_poll_and_asks_a_look, a_poll_report_makes_the_agent_present_and_births_it)
- [x] Only Ring and Bar are offered; stored `minimal` is Bar; `off`, `full` or anything else reads Ring (core/renderer/lib/termLook.ts readIndicator; termHost.ts). Flare has no Off and no Full. (M6: the_stored_word_minimal_is_bar_everything_else_ring, picks_and_the_indicator_come_from_the_look_store)
- [x] Ring (default): one mono mark at the tab's end in the close button's place, in the name's ink; spinner while working (1 s), breathing dot for question (2 s), check for finished, cross for failed (core/renderer/lib/tabMark.ts; components/TabMark.tsx; styles/marks.css). (M6: spin_is_one_second_per_turn, breathe_follows_the_cosine_keyframes; pixel tests T24, T25)
- [x] Ring stays on hover and close slides in after it; idle tab shows neither (e2e tabLooks). (M6: pixel tests T25, T31)
- [x] Bar: working is a running bar along the tab foot (1.25 s) in the accent; question, done, failed are a static 3 px line in their colour (tabMark.ts resolveTabMark; e2e indicator, attention). (M6: run_is_ease_in_out_over_1250_ms, the_line_is_three_css_pixels_snapped_at_every_scale)
- [x] The front tab never gets a finished or failed mark while looked at; marks are news (done, failed) or state (question) (agentHookState.ts raisedWhileSeen). (M6: finished_is_not_raised_on_the_tab_in_front_but_a_question_is, raised_while_seen)
- [x] Motion: question breathes 2 s; finished-with-rainbow flows (line x 8 s, y 7 s; fill 14 s); working run 1.25 s (marks.css; regression-rules finished-and-question). (M6: the_rainbow_line_flows_one_tab_in_eight_seconds, breathe_follows_the_cosine_keyframes, run_is_ease_in_out_over_1250_ms; the y 7 s and fill 14 s are Full-only and dropped with D6)
- [x] Reduced motion stills every mark; a minimised window pauses every mark (marks.css; marks.test; src/renderer/src/lib/marksPause.ts; marksPause.test). (M6: animation_effects_off_stills_every_mark, the_clock_runs_only_while_something_moves)
- [x] 18 px tab icon: Claude tab = Clawd (#d77757, 18x6 quadrant grid), codex = OpenAI mark, plain shell or other agent = prompt icon, Settings = cog (core/renderer/lib/agentIcon.ts; agentIcon.test; e2e tabLooks). (M6: agent_icons_are_eighteen_pixels, the_glyph_data_is_the_ported_artwork)
- [x] Failed tooltip: path newline "Failed: <kind words>" with the 12 kind words (rate limit, servers overloaded, sign-in failed, organisation not allowed, account on hold, billing, invalid request, model not found, server error, reply too long, cloud credentials, unknown error); unknown kind shows underscores as spaces; none shows "Failed" (tabList.ts tabTitle; agentHookSignal.ts failedLabel). (M6: failed_label_names_each_kind, the_tooltip_is_the_path_and_the_failure_under_it)

### Mark colours

- [x] Working = accent unless picked; finished = theme green floored 3:1, fallback #22c55e if under 1.15 from working; question = #3b82f6 floored; failed = first of [theme red, #ef4444, theme magenta, #d946ef] floored and apart from working and finished (src/renderer/src/lib/agentColors.ts; agentColors.test). ### Claude Code plugin and hook signal (core/claude-plugin; Flare name flare-status)

- [x] Manifest `.claude-plugin/plugin.json` name flare-status, version 1.0.0, a description (agentHookSignal.test "has a manifest Claude Code accepts"; claudePlugin.ts PLUGIN_NAME). (M6: has_a_manifest_claude_code_accepts, knows_our_plugin_by_its_manifest_name_and_nothing_else)
- [x] hooks.json has 25 command entries, each `cmd.exe /d /c call "${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/hook.cmd" <args>`, timeout 5 s, exec form: working (UserPromptSubmit, PreToolUse, PostToolUse, PostToolUseFailure, SubagentStart, PreCompact), question (PermissionRequest, Elicitation, Notification with matchers permission_prompt, elicitation_dialog, elicitation_url_dialog), done (Stop), failed with kind (StopFailure per rate_limit, overloaded, authentication_failed, oauth_org_not_allowed, account_on_hold, billing_error, invalid_request, model_not_found, server_error, max_output_tokens, cloud_credential_error, unknown), failed without kind (StopFailure *, kind "failed"); SessionStart and SessionEnd are not hooked (agentHookSignal.ts header; agentHookSignal.test). (M6: hooks_json_matches_the_table, hooks_json_has_25_entries)
- [x] hook.cmd is a static echo printing JSON `{"terminalSequence":"ESC]777;flare-agent;state=<state>[;kind=<kind>]BEL"}`, no node, no stdin; kind only on failure hooks. (M6: hook_cmd_is_crlf_and_static, every_hook_prints_what_the_parser_reads)
- [x] Only Claude's UI writes the sequence; `claude -p` and SDK runs never do (agentHookSignal.ts). (M6: T3 header and T2 parser tests)

(M6: the_plugin_folder_reaches_a_new_shell_once_after_the_users_own, an_inherited_copy_of_our_plugin_is_dropped)
- [x] Plugin dir shipped only if hooks/hooks.json exists in it (index.ts claudePluginDir); packaged under resources, dev under the repo. (M6: a_plugin_dir_needs_its_hooks_file)
- [x] A warm shell is adopted only with the same plugin key (terminal.ts pluginKey). (M6: a_warm_shell_with_the_other_plugin_answer_makes_way)
- [x] OSC 777 parser: payload max 200 chars, split on `;`, first part equals the prefix `flare-agent`, every later part contains `=` at index > 0; state in working|question|done|failed; unknown keys ignored; kind kept only with failed and matching `^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{0,39}$`; Flare must not accept `prism-agent`; non-Flare payloads leave other OSC 777 users alone (core/renderer/lib/agentHookSignal.ts; agentHookSignal.test). (M6: prism_agent_is_not_accepted, leaves_other_osc_777_users_alone)

### Hook state rules (core/renderer/lib/agentHookState.ts hookStep)

- [x] Hook working: phase working, clears question, failed, finished (hookStep). (M6: T5 hook_state tests)
- [x] Hook question: phase question, raises question, clears failed. (M6: T5 hook_state tests)
- [x] Hook done: title still spinning means phase ending (still Working); background work on screen means phase background; else finish (phase done, raises finished, clears question and failed). (M6: T5 hook_state tests, timeline_*)
- [x] Hook failed: phase failed keeping a prior kind if none, raises failed AND finished, clears question. (M6: a_new_prompt_clears_a_failure_and_the_next_has_no_old_kind)
- [x] Title spinner: while ending becomes background; while question or stopped becomes working; otherwise no change. (M6: T5 hook_state tests)
- [x] Idle star: ending or background resolves to finish or background; working becomes stopped (an Esc, no Finished); otherwise no change. (M6: timeline_b_an_esc)
- [x] Settle: no title within STOP_SETTLE_MS = 1500 ms after a spinning Stop resolves to background or finish. (M6: T5 hook_state tests)
- [x] bg-check every BACKGROUND_CHECK_MS = 3000 ms while background; finishes once neither screen nor spinner shows background work. (M6: T5 hook_state tests)
- [x] While the question box is on screen the question stands; done raises finished under the question (screenDecides). (M6: timeline_c3_the_classifier_denies, T5 hook_state tests)
- [x] After a permission Yes the spinner title returns Working; a spinner held by the box is re-read once after 250 ms (useAgentIndicator.ts titleRecheck). (M6: timeline_a_a_yes)
- [x] A hooked session's idle title after working with no Stop is Esc: idle, stopped, no Finished; a Stop landing after still finishes (agentHookState.timelines.test; e2e agentHooks). (M6: timeline_b_an_esc, timeline_d_two_prompts_in_a_row)
- [x] A hooked session is never scored from output; the screen is read only to hold a question the hooks raised (useAgentIndicator.ts onTermData). (M6: a_hooked_session_is_never_scored_and_its_screen_is_not_read_for_output)
- [x] Background work on screen: `· N shell(s)|task(s)|command(s) still running`, or below the last box rule a workflow row (the footer's `· N shell(s)|task(s)` count stopped counting 2026-10-23, regression rule 191); the workflow row is `N/M · [Xh][Ym]SSs`; only live marks count (core/renderer/lib/agentBackground.ts; agentBackground.test, screens.json; e2e backgroundWork). (M6: T6 background tests, timeline_g2_a_subagent_asks_while_its_sibling_works)
- [x] A new prompt or working signal clears failed and finished. (M6: a_new_prompt_clears_failed_finished_and_the_kind)
- [x] A question raised on the front tab; looking never takes it down (raisedWhileSeen). (M6: finished_is_not_raised_on_the_tab_in_front_but_a_question_is)

### Question detection and scoring fallback

- [x] Unhooked sessions read the bottom 16 rows: both "Enter to select" and "Esc to cancel", or "Do you want to ...?" with a "1. Yes" line (core/renderer/lib/agentQuestion.ts looksLikeQuestion; agentQuestion.test). (M6: T6 question tests)
- [x] Read 200 ms after the title goes idle and as output arrives while idle; only titled unhooked sessions; a new question cancels pending answer reads (useAgentIndicator.ts checkQuestion). (M6: T18 indicator tests)
- [x] Walking choices is not an answer; answer keys Enter, Esc, Ctrl+C, digits 1 to 9, then the box is read at 250, 1000, 2500, 5000 ms and the question clears only when the box is gone (agentQuestion.ts questionAnswered; useAgentIndicator.ts onTermKey). (M6: an_answer_key_reads_the_box_at_250_1000_2500_and_5000_and_clears_only_when_it_is_gone)
- [x] A question clears on work again, an answer key with the box gone, agent gone, tab closed, never on a look (e2e attention, agentHooks). (M6: an_answer_key_clears_a_question_when_the_box_is_gone, forget_drops_every_mark_record_and_timer)
- [x] Output scoring only for a known agent that is neither titled nor hooked; ignores startup paint, resize suppression and echo; a run with gaps <= 1500 ms longer than 1200 ms sets working; 2000 ms of silence clears it; event-driven (useAgentIndicator.ts; termActivity.ts). (M6: output_scores_working_after_a_sustained_run_and_clears_after_two_seconds_of_quiet)
- [x] A poll report that finds an agent clears the output run, marks birth, clears working. (M6: a_poll_report_makes_the_agent_present_and_births_it)

### Process poll and detection

- [x] Polls only if a pty printed since the last look or a session has no answer yet; timer 2500 ms (AGENT_POLL_MIN), wait doubles to 20000 ms while unchanged, resets on change (core/main/agentPoll.ts; agentPoll.test). (M6: T9 poll tests)
- [x] Only changes are sent; an unchanged answer is re-sent only when asked (agentPoll.ts pollAgentAgain). (M6: T9 poll tests)
- [x] Forced look (title or hook claims an agent the poll has not reported): at most once per 15 s per tab from the UI, main waits AGENT_FORCE_GAP = 5000 ms (useAgentIndicator.ts askLook; agentPoll.ts). (M6: a_claude_title_is_the_agent_present_ahead_of_the_poll_and_asks_a_look, T9 poll tests)
- [x] A query with no answer doubles to AGENT_FAIL_MAX = 60000 ms; the third failure in a row is logged once; a failed query spends no tick (agentPoll.ts). (M6: T9 poll tests)
- [x] Query: native process snapshot (pid, ppid, command line) with prefilter `claude|codex|aider|gemini` then strict match; 30 s timeout; 8 MB cap; off the UI thread (agentPoll.ts). (M6: T19 procs tests; the 30 s and 8 MB limits have no meaning for the native call, D4: each command line is capped at 32 KiB)
- [x] Forget a shell's answer when it ends; warm poll 300 ms after a spawn that really started (agentPoll.ts; ipc.ts pollAgentsSoon). (M6: `forgets_a_session_that_ended_so_its_id_starts_clean` (T9); the warm look is `started_asks_for_a_warm_look` in `agent_hub.rs` (T28), the test Appendix B called `the_poll_is_warmed_after_a_spawn`)
- [x] Signatures (agentDetect.ts AGENT_RE): `claude-code` anywhere; `claude`/`claude.exe|.cmd|.ps1` as a path segment followed by separator, quote, space or end; `codex`, `aider`, `gemini` likewise; `@openai/codex`; `gemini-cli`; case-insensitive; first token counts; a folder named Claude is not an agent; `git commit -m claude` is not (core/main/agentDetect.ts; agentDetect.test). (M6: T8 detect tests, a_title_names_the_agent_only_when_it_is_the_program)
- [x] Kind: claude wins anywhere under the shell, else codex, else other; BFS under the shell pid, cycles handled (agentDetect.ts treeAgentKind). (M6: T8 detect tests, the four-deep tree test)
- [x] Resumable kinds are claude and codex; others light the indicator but have nothing to resume (core/main/agentKinds.ts; agentKinds.test). (M6: the_resumable_agents_pass_the_prefilter)

### Storage and import

- [x] Every setting is one store with get, set, change listener; a blocked store loses the choice, never the app; unknown enum words read the default and are never written back; default-on bools read on unless '0', default-off read on only for '1'; never set is not zero (core/renderer/lib/prefStore.ts; prefStore.test; helpPrefs.test). (M7: a_blocked_store_reads_the_default_drops_writes_and_still_announces, listeners_hear_their_own_key_only_and_stop_when_cancelled, a_set_value_is_read_back_and_removed, keys_reach_the_file_byte_for_byte)
- [x] Settings write immediately and notify listeners; the window follows store changes (prefStore.ts). (M7: debounced_flush_writes_at_once, debounced_writes_the_last_save_after_the_delay, on_look_change_hears_every_look_key_and_the_draft)
- [x] Settings are written atomically (temp file then rename) in `%APPDATA%\Flare` (core/main/atomicWrite.ts; atomicWrite.test). (M7: write_atomic_replaces_and_leaves_no_temp, no_temp_files_remain, save_now_is_on_disk_and_an_older_save_never_lands_over_it)
- [x] Persisted keys carry Flare's own prefix `flare.*` with the same suffixes and meanings as Prism Terminal's `prism.*` keys: `flare.term.theme`, `font`, `fontPct`, `custom`, `seeThrough`, `agentIndicator`, `agentColor`, `agentDoneColor`, `agentQuestionColor`, `agentDoneOn`, `agentQuestionOn`, `agentRainbow`, `agentFailedOn`, `agentHooks`, `colourFormat`, `shell`; `flare.window.tabLayout`, `tabSwitch`, `edges`, `background`, `secondary`, `accent`, `customColours`, `taskbarBadge`, `agentGuard`, `agentKeys`; `flare.newtab.mode`, `folder`; `flare.help.enabled`, `shell`, `panelWidth`; `flare.recentRoots`, `pinnedRoots`; `flare.dictation.*` (M8). Prism Terminal's legacy `prism.term.acrylic` and `prism.term.opacity` are read by the first-run import only and have no `flare.*` twin. The import maps each `prism.*` key to its `flare.*` twin (O10); `prism.dictation.mic` is dropped (device ids are Chromium's), and the retired `prism.window.tabStyle`, `tabWidth` and `titleBar` are skipped. The sidebar keys `flare.window.sidebar` and `sidebarWidth` are the twins of `prism.window.sidebar` and `sidebarWidth`. (M10: the_import_map_covers_every_prism_key_and_writes_only_flare_keys, every_stored_key_starts_with_flare, crates/flare-settings/src/import/map.rs)
- [x] First-run import reads PT's Stable profile (`%APPDATA%\PrismTerminalStable`) else `%APPDATA%\PrismTerminal`, from LevelDB local storage; a value that cannot be read reliably is skipped, never guessed; settings, themes, custom theme, fonts and shells import once; tabs do not (design spec Migration). (M7: imports_once, stable_wins_when_it_has_keys, main_is_used_when_stable_is_absent_or_empty, a_pt_stable_profile_becomes_a_flare_profile_before_the_first_paint, tabs_are_never_imported)
- [x] Indicator `off`/`full` import as Ring, `minimal` as Bar (termLook.ts). (M7: indicator_words)
- [x] Legacy keys prism.term.opacity and prism.term.acrylic are read once and removed (seeThroughMigration.test, opacityMigration.test). (M7: migrate_runs_opacity_then_see_through_and_leaves_no_legacy_key, is_idempotent_a_second_run_changes_nothing, legacy_opacity_is_folded_and_the_legacy_keys_are_never_written)
- [x] Explorer verb state lives in the registry, not the settings file. (M10: the_row_reads_the_registry_not_the_marker, set_answers_what_the_registry_says_afterwards, crates/flare-win/src/verb_switch.rs)

## M8: Dictation (M8 target: Prism Terminal main e5848cb2c2f7874439b286f68aad8b60f02ff1d9, 2026-10-17)

Scope is v0.55.6 dictation: Parakeet, warm-up and the GPU pack are in; dictate-anywhere is out. AMENDED at e5848cb: dictate from any app is in (N12 to N28); the M8 target is Prism Terminal main e5848cb, not v0.55.6 (see the "Since v0.55.6" lines at the end of this section).

### Off, on, master switch

- [x] Dictation is off by default; no key listener, no process, no download until switched on (core/renderer/lib/dictationPrefs.ts dictationEnabled; dictation.ts "Off means off"). (M8: dictation_is_off_by_default_nothing_starts, crates/flare-dictation/src/controller.rs; defaults_are_off_hold_ctrl_win_auto_sounds_on_anywhere_on, crates/flare-dictation/src/prefs.rs)
- [x] Master row dictation-enabled "Use dictation", "Speech to text on this PC, never sent."; with the switch on and no model installed the sub becomes the warning "Needs a speech model before it works." (dictationOptions.ts; DictationPage.tsx noModelYet). (M8 e2e: dictation, tools/e2e/src/scenarios/dictation.rs; M8: the_switches_and_the_choices_write_their_own_keys, crates/flare/src/dictation_ctl.rs)
- [x] Switching off during a recording cancels it (nothing pasted) and stops the speech server and media helper (dictation.ts armDictation.sync; dictationIpc.ts DCH.stop). (M8: switching_off_mid_recording_pastes_nothing_and_stops, crates/flare-dictation/src/controller.rs)
- [x] With dictation off the key does nothing and no whisper-server or parakeet-cli process exists (e2e dictation, dictationParakeet). (M8: with_dictation_off_the_key_does_nothing, crates/flare-dictation/src/controller.rs; M8 e2e: dictation and dictationParakeet, tools/e2e/src/scenarios/dictation.rs)
- [x] On arm the engine is warmed after 3000 ms the first time, 500 ms on re-syncs; asked again at each press and when the model or language changes; warm is a no-op with no installed model (dictation.ts warmEngine, begin; dictationIpc.ts DCH.warm). (M8: warm_after_3000_ms_the_first_time_and_500_ms_on_re_syncs, warm_is_asked_again_at_each_press, warm_is_asked_again_when_the_model_or_language_changes, warm_never_with_no_model, crates/flare-dictation/src/controller.rs)
- [x] Quit kills every dictation child (a Job Object with kill-on-close) (dictationIpc.ts disposer; index.ts stopDictation). (M8: quit_stops_every_child_first, crates/flare/src/dictation_ctl.rs; a_child_in_the_job_dies_with_the_job, crates/flare-dictation/src/job.rs)

(M8: type_checked_pastes_then_presses_enter_only_if_the_tab_can_still_take_it, type_checked_never_presses_enter_after_words_that_were_not_written, type_checked_types_nothing_where_windows_says_no_claude_runs, type_checked_types_nothing_when_the_tab_changed_while_windows_was_asked, type_checked_holds_the_enter_back_when_a_question_opened_before_it, paste_of_frames_the_text_as_one_paste_with_every_escape_and_control_taken_out, decide_into_sends_to_the_picked_tab_only_while_its_claude_can_take_it, crates/flare-dictation/src/route.rs; heard_text_and_messages_are_capped, crates/flare-dictation/src/service.rs; DIFF-23 for the refused write)
- [x] **N25** The tab's Claude session: the `flare-status` plugin's UserPromptSubmit and Stop hooks pass `+sid`, and `hook.cmd` appends `;session=<CLAUDE_CODE_SESSION_ID>` read with delayed expansion only when it is 36 characters of `0-9 a-f -` (anything else is left out and the state still prints); the parser carries `session` only as a lower-case UUID, the state stands without it; a tab knows its current session from those hooks, and a restored tab from its resume id (agentHookSignal.ts; hook.cmd; App.tsx ledgerLink). (M8: carries_a_session_id_only_as_a_uuid_and_keeps_the_state_without_one, only_the_turns_own_two_hooks_name_the_session, crates/flare-agent/src/signal.rs; every_hook_prints_what_the_parser_reads, crates/flare-agent/src/plugin.rs; a_tab_learns_its_session_from_the_turn_hooks, a_restored_tab_takes_its_session_from_the_resume_id, crates/flare/src/agent_hub.rs; DIFF-21 (the session goes at the next prompt, PROPOSED))
- [x] **N26** One dictation for every Flare copy: a local pipe `\\.\pipe\flare-dictation-<first 24 hex of sha256(key)>` with a current-user DACL; the key is 32 random bytes as hex in `<dictation root>\link.key`, created exclusively so two copies agree; a file there that is not 64 hex digits (emptied or cut short by a crash) is replaced atomically by a fresh key (audit 2026-10-20 L38, was left alone so the link stayed off for good); a key that can be neither read nor made means no link. Handshake: service sends a nonce; the client answers `hello` with its pid, name, its nonce and `HMAC(key, "client|" + service nonce + "|" + client nonce)`; the service answers `proof` with `HMAC(key, "service|" + client nonce + "|" + service nonce)`; a peer that fails, or has not proved itself in 5000 ms, is dropped and answered nothing. One JSON message per line, at most 64 KiB: a line or an unfinished buffer over 64 KiB drops the peer, and a reply is taken only from the peer that was asked (DIFF-22); text at most 8000; at most 64 tabs per copy, ids and tab ids 128, labels 256, folders 1024; a tab list asked with an 800 ms limit (a copy that does not answer is left out); a send with 15000 ms ("that copy of the app did not answer"); a gone copy "that copy of the app has closed"; a client retries every 1000 ms. Under `--e2e` only the scenario's own pipe and key (`FLARE_E2E_DICT_PIPE`, `FLARE_E2E_DICT_KEY`), else no link (dictationLink.ts; src/main/index.ts dictationLinkConfig; DIFF-20 for Flare's own pipe). (M8: the_key_is_made_once_and_the_pipe_is_named_by_its_hash, two_copies_starting_together_agree_on_one_key, a_peer_that_fails_the_proof_is_answered_nothing_and_dropped, a_peer_that_proves_nothing_in_time_is_dropped, a_line_over_64_kib_drops_the_peer, a_buffer_that_never_ends_a_line_drops_the_peer_too, a_second_service_on_the_same_name_is_refused, the_caps_of_a_tab_list, crates/flare-dictation/tests/link.rs; DIFF-20 for Flare's own pipe; DIFF-22 for the strictness)
- [x] **N27** The list across copies: this copy's tabs first, then each linked copy's with ids `<pid>:<id>`; copy names shown only when more than one copy is open, a second of the same name numbered ("Flare (2)"); rows grouped by copy in the order the copies appear, numbered 1 to 9 across the groups, a header per group only when more than one group has sessions; a folder shown by its last two parts (dictateAnywhere.ts mergeTabs, copyNames, pickRows, folderTail; D8). (M8: merge_tabs_numbers_a_second_copy_of_the_same_name, merge_tabs_lists_every_copy_naming_the_app_only_when_there_is_more_than_one, pick_rows_keeps_each_copy_together_numbered_across_them, pick_rows_draws_no_headers_when_every_session_is_in_one_copy, folder_tail_shows_the_last_two_parts, crates/flare-dictation/src/route.rs; asks_a_connected_copy_for_its_tabs_sends_into_one_and_forgets_it_when_it_closes, crates/flare-dictation/tests/link.rs)
- [x] **N28** The service ending: a waiting copy's hook takes the lock within its 500 ms poll, becomes the service, and lists its own tabs (e2e dictateTwoCopies). (M8: the_service_closing_hands_over_to_a_waiting_copy, crates/flare-dictation/src/service.rs; a_client_finds_the_service_when_it_starts_later, crates/flare-dictation/tests/link.rs; M8 e2e: dictateTwoCopies, tools/e2e/src/scenarios/anywhere.rs)

## M9: Updates, installer, Windows integration, diagnostics

### Update check

- [x] The check runs only in a packaged build; dev and test builds never touch the network; the unpackaged build shows the preview (src/main/update.ts watchForUpdates; core/main/updatePreview.ts). The whole updater stays off until the repo is public (design spec). (M9: a_default_build_has_no_live_mode, off_and_preview_make_no_checks_and_no_installs, no_network_source_exists_without_the_feature, is_packaged_wants_current_and_update_exe)
- [x] First check at launch then every 4 hours (CHECK_EVERY_MS), timer does not keep the process alive, offline is silent; each check writes a crumb `update-check` with offered, fetchMs, offerMs, heapMb, rssMb (update.ts; docs/diagnostics.md). (M9: live_checks_at_launch_then_every_four_hours, each_check_leaves_a_crumb, offline_is_silent, the_thread_does_not_keep_the_process_alive)
- [x] No offer unless the version is strictly newer (newerVersion, non-numeric parts count 0); release body kept raw, cut to 20,000 chars; the installer asset is found by an anchored name pattern that the sibling app's name cannot match (update.ts latestUpdate, isInstallerName; update.test). DECISION: Velopack feed replaces the GitHub asset scan (open decision O15). RESOLVED in M9 (O15: the Velopack feed replaces the asset scan; offers_a_newer_release, offers_nothing_when_not_newer_or_empty_or_failed, accepts_a_v_prefix)
- [x] A download URL is accepted only if https, host github.com, under `/<repo>/releases/download/`, ends `.exe`, no credentials, query or hash (update.ts isReleaseAssetUrl). DECISION: re-expressed as "only the package Velopack offered". RESOLVED in M9 (re-expressed as only the package Velopack offered, D2: installs_only_the_current_offer, refuses_a_package_with_another_id, rejects_lookalikes)
- [x] Offer shape `{version, url, notes, mock?}` (core/shared/updateTypes.ts). (M9: re-expressed, D2: Offer {version, notes, mock, token}; set_offer_takes_a_fresh_token_and_counts_no_check)

### Privacy statement (PRIVACY.md)

- [x] No accounts, analytics, telemetry or crash reporting; one automatic request, the update check, and it is off until the repo is public (state this when it is switched on). (M9: privacy::no_telemetry_words, the_update_check_sentence_says_off)
- [ ] Only on request: installing an update downloads the release; dictation (off by default) downloads engine and model from pinned sources with SHA-256 checks and sends voice only to loopback or the engine's input; links open only on click; Sonara question and tab id go only to 127.0.0.1; the Claude plugin makes no network call and is passed by environment, never written into the user's Claude Code settings; OSC 52 copy up to 1 MB with no read answered; XTVERSION answers only the asker; the log holds no typed text, clipboard, audio, screen text, title text or conversation (PRIVACY.md).
- [x] PRIVACY.md changes in the same PR whenever a network request is added. (M9: privacy::a_new_file_with_a_network_call_fails_the_scan)

### Icon and brand

- [x] One .ico for app, installer, uninstaller and taskbar, generated from square artwork on a rounded black square (inset round(8*n/256), corner radius 56/256) with a #202020 one-pixel outline, 8x supersampling, LANCZOS; frames 16, 20, 24, 28, 30, 32, 36, 40, 42, 48, 54, 56, 60, 64, 72, 80, 96, 128, 256 (tools/make-icon.py). Superseded by O17 (2026-10-17): the owner's artwork is rendered as it is, with no badge and no outline.
- [ ] The window icon is the exe's own; the badge stays an overlay.
- [x] DECISION: Flare artwork reuse or new (O17). Answered 2026-10-17: the owner's artwork (assets/icon/flare-color.svg) is the app icon and the in-app mark, used as it is.

## M10: Parity sweep, e2e, performance

### Harness rules

- [x] The e2e drives the built release exe and exits 1 with "run build first" when missing; `FLARE_E2E_PACKAGED=1` drives the installed layout (tools/e2e/run.mjs:24-33). (M10: `launch_adds_the_e2e_flag_and_never_foregrounds`, tools/e2e/tests/harness.rs; the agent verdicts are read per tab by `wait_for_agent_verdict`, tools/e2e/src/world.rs)

(DIFFERENCE DIFF-13: parked at (-32000, -32000) with no opacity change; `park_moves_the_window_off_every_monitor`, crates/flare-win/src/window_park.rs; `the_foreground_guard_flags_a_window_of_the_run_and_only_notes_another_app`, tools/e2e/tests/world.rs)

- [x] Reap after every scenario and once before the first: kill every process whose command line contains the profile name, tree and all; never the owner's own Flare (run.mjs:52-80). (M10: `the_reaper_spares_a_process_without_the_world_path`, tools/e2e/tests/world.rs)

## Decisions (2026-10-17)

Settled by the builder under the owner's standing rule (obvious picks decided, parity by default);
O17's artwork arrived 2026-10-17 (the owner's own icon).

- O1 the cd line: KEEP for parity (`termCwd.ts cdCommand`). The design's "only command is the
  resume" is read as Prism Terminal's actual rule, which includes the idle-prompt cd line.
- O2 physical keys: the builder of M2 reads physical keys (scan codes) through the window's native
  key events (winit `KeyCode` or Win32), never a limitation; Ctrl+C/V and the dictation key match
  the physical key.
- O3 cursor-line carry: RESOLVED in M2: no cursor-line carry; vertical re-anchor, see features.md.
- O4 Explorer verb: "Open in Flare" (both apps can be installed, so the label must say which).
- O5 resume shells: parity, no new shells. M6: handled here; a warm shell is never adopted for a resume (`a_warm_shell_is_never_adopted_for_a_resume`), and the resume is only the startup command of a restored agent tab.
- O6 tab id: `FLARE_TAB_ID` only; Sonara learns it in its own repo later (raised with the owner then). M6: handled here; Flare sets `FLARE_TAB_ID` and posts `host_tab` with it; Sonara reads `PRISM_TAB_ID` until it learns the new name (owner question 3 below).
- O7 eyedropper: ported in M7 with a Win32 screen pick (parity). Ported in M7, not moved to M10: `flare-win/src/eyedrop.rs` and the picker tests hold it; the by-hand window test (`the_window_picks_a_pixel`) was not run for this PR (it needs someone at the desktop to click).
- O8 See-through sub: keep Prism Terminal's text verbatim (parity beats the copy rule here).
- O9 About line: "Flare <version>. Made with Slint." plus the copyright line from LICENSE.
- O10 setting keys: `flare.*` inside Flare, with an import map from Prism Terminal's `prism.*` keys.
- O11 help panel close slide: 220 ms, as the code does.
- O12 Settings: a tab, as in Prism Terminal.
- O13 dictation start cue: do what Prism Terminal v0.55.6 does (parity).
- O14 dictation models: Flare's own `%LOCALAPPDATA%\FlareDictation`; on first use it reuses models
  already in `%LOCALAPPDATA%\PrismDictation` when their pinned checksums match (copy or hard link),
  so nobody downloads 3 GB twice. Flare never writes into Prism's folder.
- O15 Velopack: one-click per-user install, releases feed on GitHub Releases, tags `v<version>`;
  the update chip and window stay as the UI (off until the repo is public).
- O16 diagnostics: a native stall watchdog (UI thread heartbeat) and timing of slow work replace
  the page-stack and IPC timings; M9 builder's call within that.
- O17 icon: answered 2026-10-17. The owner's artwork (assets/icon/flare-color.svg) is the app icon and the in-app mark; the placeholder is dropped;
  XTVERSION reports `Flare <version>`.
- O18 rows: port the shipped v0.55.6 build, not Prism Terminal's spec.
- O19 media pause: native GSMTC through the `windows` crate; unzip with a Rust zip crate. Both
  recorded in README and THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES when added.
- O20 rename: not in Prism Terminal, so not in Flare; Explorer verbs are M9.
- O21 Git Bash: parity, not detected.
- O22 click-to-open: an ALLOWLIST, not the Prism Terminal denylist (safer than parity). Only known
  document, text, data, image, audio, video and non-running source types open; every other type is
  revealed in Explorer, never opened. Trailing dot or space segments and `:` streams are refused,
  and the extension is read from the canonicalised path. Folders keep opening. Rule 134.
- O23 selection on a redrawn screen (M5 plan D2, 2026-10-17): alacritty clears a selection when the
  program erases the lines it touches (EL, ED) and on a column-count resize; xterm.js kept it. A
  selection in scrollback is unaffected; on a live screen a program that redraws (Claude Code) can
  lose it while it streams. Known divergence. Owner question 1 answered by the orchestrator: fix in
  M10 (keep the selection while a program redraws). Test: `an_erase_over_the_selection_clears_it`.
- O24 link click (M5 plan D6, 2026-10-17): a link opens on a plain left click, as Prism Terminal
  (two-apps #95); a right click opens nothing. Owner question 2 answered by the orchestrator: keep
  the plain click for every link, path links included (`link_click_needs_ctrl` returns false; one
  line to change). Tests: `any_other_button_opens_nothing`, `a_path_is_a_link_only_if_it_exists_and_opens_by_the_allowlist`.
- O25 Alt+click moving the caret (M5 plan D8, 2026-10-17): PORTED. The planner left it out (D8);
  owner question 5 was answered "include" by the orchestrator (parity: Prism Terminal leaves
  xterm's `altClickMovesCursor` on), so the final review ported xterm's `moveToCellSequence` cell
  for cell (`flare-term/src/alt_click.rs`, with xterm's `MoveToCell.test.ts` table). Prism
  Terminal's own click-to-caret still refuses any modifier. Tests: `xterm_normal_buffer_table`,
  `an_alt_click_moves_the_cursor_with_arrows_and_selects_nothing`.
- O26 empty cell and typed space (M5 plan D10, 2026-10-17): the buffer adapter reads an empty cell
  and a typed space as the same character (alacritty stores a space for both), so a prompt's
  trailing space is not "printed text" for the click-to-caret end and for selection trimming. No
  owner question. Test: `nothing_deletable_is_allowed_and_empty`.
- O27 multi-line paste (M5 plan D15, 2026-10-17): no confirmation dialog and no "paste is large"
  notice, as Prism Terminal has none. Bracketed paste is the multi-line safety; a shell that did not
  ask for it receives the lines, as in xterm. Owner question 4 answered by the orchestrator: keep
  parity, no confirmation (a paste is the user's own act). Test: `a_multi_line_paste_is_one_bracketed_block`.
- O28 (M9 D1, 2026-10-17): the updater is a compile-time cargo feature `live-updates`, off by default; the owner flips it with one reviewed line, a PRIVACY.md edit and a release; no per-user toggle. Test: `a_default_build_has_no_live_mode`.
- O29 (M9 D2): the offer carries an opaque token, never a download URL; install runs only for the current token, and a feed refuses a version it did not find. Test: `refuses_any_other_token_a_none_and_everything_with_no_offer`.
- O30 (M9 D3): the verb registry is written through `RegCreateKeyExW` and friends, never `reg.exe`; no process is spawned. Test: `registry` tests in flare-win.
- O31 (M9 D4): the verb points at `<install root>\current\Flare.exe`; `current` keeps its name across updates, so the verb survives an update. Test: `verb_exe_is_the_current_folder_exe`.
- O32 (M9 D5): a verb write is allowed only when packaged and not under `--e2e`; under `--e2e` with a sandbox root the same code writes under `Software\FlareE2E\`. Test: `verb_switch` tests.
- O33 (M9 D6): cancel is cooperative; the abandoned download finishes in the background and its files are deleted, and a busy feed refuses a new download. Test: `cancel_wins_even_after_the_last_byte`.
- O34 (M9 D7): the release notes come from the feed (`vpk pack --releaseNotes`), cut to 20,000 units and parsed by the ported parser, never rendered. Test: `carries_the_notes_raw`.
- O35 (M9 D8): the vpk flags live in `tools/release/pack.ps1`: one-click, per-user, Start menu shortcut only, unsigned, win-x64. Test: `release_files` tests.
- O36 (M9 D9): "packaged" means installed by Velopack (folder `current`, parent holds `Update.exe`, no `.portable` marker). Test: `is_packaged_wants_current_and_update_exe`.
- O37 (M9 D10): auto-apply is off; the uninstall hook removes the two verb keys read from `verb_keys()`; `run()` is the first statement of `main`; under `--e2e` the hook touches only `Software\FlareE2E\`. Test: `bootstrap_turns_auto_apply_off`.
- O38 (M9 D11): preview mode is `--preview-update` (also in an unpackaged build that is not a macOS `.app` bundle, rule 1128, and under `--e2e`); live mode needs packaged, not e2e, and the feature. Test: `mode_table`.
- O39 (M9 D12, O16 resolved): the native diagnostics kinds replace the page kinds (see `docs/diagnostics.md`); `mark` carries no note, so the log holds no typed text. Test: `a_mark_carries_no_note`.
- O40 (M9 D13): the reader is the cargo alias `cargo diag`, with `--dir` and `--profile` in place of PT's `--app`. Test: `kinds_filter_and_n_limit`.
- O41 (M9 D14, Q1 open): one-click setup with no licence page, as Velopack has none; the owner may ask for a licence screen later.
- O42 (M9 D15): release identity is tag `v<version>`; a version already released publishes nothing and says so, and is not replaced in place (not ported, the PT line is resolved this way).
- O43 (M9 D16, superseded by O17 on 2026-10-17): the placeholder icon generator was never needed; the owner's artwork is the icon, and `tools/make-icon.py` renders it.
- M3 owner decisions (2026-10-17, from the M3 plan): O12 Settings is a tab, as in Prism Terminal;
  O20 no rename (not in Prism Terminal, so not in Flare; the DECISION line above is answered);
  O10 setting keys are `flare.*` with the import map (M7 builds the import).
- M3 planner decisions D1 to D14 (taken by the builder, for the owner to overrule; the plan's
  "Decisions taken by the planner" section holds the full text): D1 the `flare-tabs` crate holds the
  pure tab logic; D2 `PrefStore` in `flare-settings`; D3 `window.json` logical size, physical
  position, saved scale; D4 single instance by a named mutex and pipe keyed by the profile hash;
  D5 tab geometry in device pixels in Rust; D6 the +N button is a fixed 72 px box; D7 the close
  question names keep the brighter ink, without the 2 px backdrop blur; D8 hairlines are
  `max(1, floor(scale))` device pixels; D9 the terminal keeps its size while the sidebar slides; D10
  marks and agent icons are M6's; D11 the Settings tab body is a placeholder until M7; D12 a folder
  dropped on the tab row or the sidebar opens a new tab; D13 `--user-data-dir` and `--e2e` are parsed
  now; D14 placeholders of a tab coming back to an agent show the bare ground.
- Owner questions raised by M3 (none blocks it): Q1 the close question has no 2 px backdrop blur
  behind the scrim (Slint has none), and the names keep the brighter ink; Q2 the +N button is a fixed
  72 px box (D6), say if it should be content-sized; Q3 the Ctrl+W label reads "Ctrl+W" (the
  Prism Terminal menu said Ctrl+Shift+W); both keys close the tab, and Ctrl+W is assumed.

M4 owner questions (none blocks M4; recorded by the M4 planner, 2026-10-17):
- Q1 See-through on the software renderer (D5): it stays solid and says so. A second presenter (a layered window with per-pixel alpha) would give it glass but replaces Slint's presenter; say if you want that in M10.
- Q2 Windows 11 21H2 (D6): the material needs 22H2, so 21H2 reads "Needs Windows 11 22H2." Say if 21H2 matters.
- Q3 The reference app is Prism Terminal 0.58.0, not 0.55.6 (D14). Differences between them are recorded as version drift, not as defects.
- Q4 The transparent DirectComposition swapchain (D8) is used from start on Windows 11 with the GPU renderer so the slider works without a restart. Say if you would rather have glass only after a restart.

M4 planner decisions D1 to D16 (taken by the builder, for the owner to overrule; the M4 plan's "Decisions taken by the planner" section holds the full text):
- D1 fixtures come from Prism Terminal's code, not typed numbers: `tools/oracle` writes the fixtures and the generated `presets_data.rs` and `fonts_data.rs`.
- D2 `flare-theme` is pure and depends on nothing; the `TermTheme` to `Palette` conversion lives in the binary (`chrome_apply::palette_of`).
- D3 all of Prism Terminal's `colour.test.ts` ports into `flare-theme` (colourCommit, alphaPct and snapAlphaStep too); `seeThrough.test` ports there. The import migrations, `colourFormat` and `termPrefs` stay in M7.
- D4 the ground is painted by the UI when the window is see-through (`paintsGround`); at 100 percent the grid paints its own opaque ground.
- D5 see-through needs the GPU renderer; the software presenter has no alpha, so the window stays solid there (Q1).
- D6 two Windows gates: `acrylic_ok` (build 22000) decides the "Windows 11" text; `system_backdrop_ok` (build 22621) lets Flare make the DWM call (Q2).
- D7 material before colour, natively: going glass calls the backdrop first, then lowers the UI ground alpha; leaving glass raises the UI first, then removes the backdrop.
- D8 the window is created transparent whenever glass could ever be used (GPU and the 22H2 gate); the ground stays opaque until the slider moves (Q4).
- D9 theme state lives in `flare-settings::look::LookStore` over M3's `PrefStore`, under `flare.*` keys (O10); the editor draft is memory only.
- D10 a Custom with a sixteen-colour entry missing draws that slot in `pt-default`'s colour.
- D11 a terminal face is the first installed face of its stack; a stack with no installed face falls to the first monospace face quietly.
- D12 chrome tokens stay Prism Terminal's strings (`--p-...`) inside `flare-theme`; `chrome_apply` parses them once. The Slint property is the token name without `--p-` (`ground` and `ground-solid` are the two exceptions).
- D13 a developer way to wear a theme until M7: `--theme`, `--see-through`, `--font`, `--font-pct`, `--accent`, `--background`, `--secondary`, `--edges`; F9 and Shift+F9 walk the wall, F8 steps the see-through by 10 (both with the F12 overlay open). They write the store.
- D14 reference screenshots come from the installed Prism Terminal 0.58.0, driven by Playwright in a throwaway profile (Q3).
- D15 no `regex` crate in `flare-theme`; the parsers are hand-written and tested against the 28 parse vectors.
- D16 `edge_for` (the DWM border colour) lives in `flare-win::window_edge`, reading the step table from `flare-theme::edges`.

M6 owner questions (none blocks M6; recorded by the M6 builder, 2026-10-17):
- Q1 Tab-icon artwork: the OpenAI mark (Simple Icons 15, CC0) for Codex and the Clawd pixel art in `#d77757` for Claude ship in this private repo, named in `THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md` under Bundled artwork. Say if either must go.
- Q2 The taskbar disc counts Finished, Question and Failed tabs exactly as Prism Terminal does, and is on by default. Confirm it stays on. No answer is recorded yet.
- Q3 Sonara still reads `PRISM_TAB_ID` (O6). Until Sonara learns `FLARE_TAB_ID`, a Flare tab's question is not read aloud. Raise it in Sonara's repo when M6 ships.
- The planner decisions D1 to D16 for the owner to overrule are in the M6 entry of `docs/features.md`.

## Deliberate differences

Each row is a recorded difference from Prism Terminal that a parity line may be ticked with (`DIFF-<n>`). The
"Rests on" cell names the owner decision or planner decision it stands on; a row without one fails `parity_lines.rs`.
A PROPOSED row awaits the owner's yes and is repeated in the PR description (M10 plan, Questions Q2).

| Id | Prism Terminal does | Flare does | Rests on | State |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| DIFF-1 | Custom title bar on the DWM frame, never frameless (parity M1) | A frameless Slint window (`no-frame: true`) with the DWM backdrop called on it; the M4 spike showed no DWM border and working acrylic | M1 design (Slint draws all chrome), `docs/material.md` Decisions ("DWM border visible on the frameless window: no") | PROPOSED |
| DIFF-2 | The two-path app mark in the title bar | The owner's flame mark, tinted like the old one | Owner, 2026-10-17, answers O17 and ICON update in the follow-ups queue | confirmed |
| DIFF-3 | 2 px backdrop blur behind scrims and menus | None (Slint has no backdrop blur) | M3 Q1, M7 D9 | PROPOSED |
| DIFF-4 | ClearType fringes, Chromium's hinting and 3-device-pixel hairlines at 225% | Greyscale text, 2 device-pixel hairlines at 225% by `Px.hair()` | M7 image review, M3 D8 | PROPOSED |
| DIFF-5 | See-through glass on its renderer | See-through needs the GPU renderer; the software renderer stays solid and says so | M4 Q1 (owner has not answered) | PROPOSED |
| DIFF-6 | Click-to-open denylist | Allowlist | O22 | confirmed (rule 134) |
| DIFF-7 | Keys `prism.*` | Keys `flare.*` with an import map | O10, M3 D2 | confirmed |
| DIFF-8 | Explorer verb label "Open terminal here" | "Open in Flare" | O4 | confirmed |
| DIFF-9 | Page-stack and IPC timing in the diagnostics log | A native UI-thread watchdog and work timers | O16 | confirmed |
